notes = "标题：MOSAIC: Memory-Organized Search with Anti-Imitation Crossover for Training-Free Microarchitecture Optimization\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31733\n摘要：Large language models can generate cache-replacement policies. The central challenge is to retain performance on workloads excluded from the search. In our adaptations of published search methods, generated code resembles established policies, with cosine similarities of 0.78-0.84. The wider comparison also shows held-out harmonic-mean instructions-per-cycle (IPC) losses of 1.0-2.4%. We present MOSAIC, a search controller that improves policies without updating language-model weights. It stores concise lessons from previous evaluations rather than repeatedly copying high-scoring code. When improvement stops, it selects two high-scoring policies whose code embeddings differ and asks the model to combine their mechanisms. Novelty prompts and model rotation support this search; controlled mem"

notes = "标题：Eliciting sandbagged LLM capability via structural interventions on attention-head functional networks\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31615\n摘要：Reliably evaluating the capabilities of advanced AI systems is critical for safe development and deployment, yet such evaluations may be undermined by sandbagging, a situation in which a model strategically suppresses capabilities it possesses. content = "- **问题**：离散偏微分方程的线性求解器高度依赖专家逐题配置，需要自动发现兼顾收敛性、精度和速度等指标的方案。\n- **旧法为何失效**：传统调优通常只返回单个求解器；单智能体在声散射和弹性任务中无法生成可执行方案，Jacobi预条件Krylov基线在耦合动态热弹性问题上甚至无法收敛。\n- **方法**：让语言模型智能体分别提出、批评、实现和评估由Krylov方法、预条件器、块分解及神经算子组成的元求解器，并通过非支配排序遗传算法沿六项实测指标进化候选群体，形成帕累托前沿。系统使用单台笔记本上运行的小型开放权重模型。\n- **实验**：在四类PDE问题上对比单智能体和Jacobi预条件Krylov基线；三维声散射与弹性任务的60个候选中分别有47和48个收敛，最佳方案将迭代次数降低4.7倍和60倍、在线求解时间降低2.3倍和3.0倍。二维和三维动态热弹性任务完成100个瞬态候选中的98个，分别以快15倍的速度达到同等精度、将位移误差降低20倍。"

For three-dimensional acoustic scattering and elasticity, it delivers 47 and"

content = "- **问题**：自主编程与科研智能体通过实验决定下一步行动，但实验结果往往含噪且不确定，难以用真实标签判断每次决策是否改善目标。\n- **旧法为何失效**：传统评测依赖证明正确性、代码编译和单元测试等外部真值，无法覆盖动态生成假设、提前终止及相互依赖的开放式决策轨迹。\n- **方法**：将智能体质量定义为轨迹中正确决策的加权和，以e值序列作为正确性的统计证据，并构造质量的上下界估计量与置信集；框架支持任意依赖决策、数据依赖式停止和事后定义质量指标。\n- **实验**：通过优化一个开源Python包运行时间的案例比较Claude Opus与Claude Haiku，并证明估计保证对不同轨迹分布一致成立，估计量类别具有实例级锐性。"

notes = "标题：Agent Evaluation Without Ground Truth: A Null Proportion Perspective\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31539\n摘要：The correctness of actions taken by agents is typically evaluated against known, externally verified ground truth labels, such as whether a proof is correct or whether code compiles and passes unit tests. score = 3

summary = "用无需金标的变形关系，检测概率决策模型在改写、否定和选项变化下是否保持自洽。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31533"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：概率决策模型面对同一输入的改写、否定、组合、选项变化或批次变化时，输出概率是否仍遵守概率与选择规律。\n- **旧法为何失效**：准确率无法揭示概率分布的内部矛盾；高准确率模型仍可能严重违反互补事件概率和为一等基本规律。\n- **方法**：JevBench 无需金标，通过50种变形关系从表述、批次独立性、概率测度、逻辑顺序和选择集条件化五个维度测试一致性；JevBench-240含12个决策领域、240个合成案例和12,000项测试。\n- **实验**：21个开放发布模型的一致性得分为56.5至83.9；1,200项测试的JevBench-mini与完整套件排名高度一致，Spearman相关系数达0.983。均匀参考模型虽忽略输入、准确率仅为随机水平，却取得89.4分并超过所有受测模型；解码器准确率为82.4%至89.2%，一致性仅为64.2至83.9。"
purpose = "在概率分类、风险判断或等级决策系统中，将JevBench作为无需标注的可靠性评测套件，补充准确率评估并定位概率规律违例。"
insight = "预测正确不代表概率判断自洽，甚至忽略输入的均匀分布也可能比高准确率模型更遵守概率规律，因此决策模型必须同时评估准确性与一致性。"
notes = "标题：JevBench: Metamorphic Coherence Testing for Typed Probabilistic Decision Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31533\n摘要：Typed probabilistic decision models return distributions over specified answers, such as yes or no, a set of options or levels on an ordered scale. We ask whether their probabilities remain coherent when questions about the same input are reworded, negated, combined, given different options or placed in different batches. JevBench tests this coherence without gold labels. Its 50 metamorphic relations each test one law of probability or choice across five dimensions: representation, batch independence, probability measure, logical order and choice-set conditioning. Each test changes the question or batch while keeping the input fixed. Every model receives the same requests, fixed in advance. JevBench-240 contains 12,000 tests across 240 synthetic cases in 12 decision domains. A fixed subset"

Specifically, we show that during the continual learning process, the OOD capabilities of the model degrade, following a shared representational pattern across domain shifts, which is also visible behaviorally in the two simpler suites. content = "- **问题**：空间推理智能体需要编排三维重建、分割和几何计算等工具，并正确决定调用对象、执行顺序及结果解释方式。\n- **旧法为何失效**：无状态智能体无法积累历史交互中的有效策略，会在不同任务中反复犯下相同的工具编排错误。\n- **方法**：SpatialCraft从同一任务的对比轨迹中提炼条件—动作式Experiences，并维护带有明确激活和终止条件的多步Skills；系统通过随机屏蔽经验计算Contrastive Utility Score，将持续有效的经验锚定并整合进对应技能。\n- **实验**：在5个空间推理基准与4种骨干模型组成的20项设置中，SpatialCraft均取得最高准确率。"

notes = "标题：SpatialCraft: Evolving Procedural Intelligence from Experience and Skills for Spatial Reasoning Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31475\n摘要：Spatial reasoning agents increasingly rely on external tools, such as 3D reconstruction, segmentation, and geometric computation, to answer questions that a multimodal large language model (MLLM) cannot resolve from pixels alone. Tool access, however, shifts the bottleneck to orchestration: the agent must decide which tools to call, in what order, and how to interpret their outputs, yet a stateless agent repeats the same mistakes across tasks. We ask whether a frozen tool-using agent can improve its orchestration from its own interaction history, without parameter updates. We present SpatialCraft, a self-evolving framework that maintains two forms of external knowledge: Experiences, condition-action tactics distilled by contrasting rollouts of the same task, and Skills, multi-step procedur"


[[items]]
id = "harness-rehearsal"

content = "- **问题**：智能体失败可能源于缺少任务知识，也可能源于不会通过特定脚手架的提示、动作格式和反馈循环表达已有知识，需要将任务能力与接口能力分开衡量和训练。\n- **旧法为何失效**：单脚手架训练虽能增加任务知识，却会干扰其他接口上的能力，甚至导致留出脚手架的性能灾难性下降。\n- **方法**：提出 harness rehearsal（接口演练），用任务知识含量极低但遵循目标接口的轨迹单独训练接口能力；同时以多脚手架蒸馏汇聚任务知识，从而维持推理时的接口能力。\n- **实验**：在四个推理与智能体基准上验证；Qwen3-4B 在 BFCL V3 多轮任务中，跨脚手架任务监督与单独接口演练分别达到 8.5% 和 32.5%，结合后达到 58.8%。在策略强化学习中，该方法比单脚手架蒸馏更少损害其他接口，且蒸馏获得的接口能力可在后续强化学习中保留。"
purpose = "在需要更换提示模板、动作格式或工具协议的智能体系统中，可借鉴接口演练与多脚手架蒸馏来迁移任务知识并维持目标接口性能。"
insight = "任务知识不会自动转化为可执行表现，推理时接口能力是知识能否兑现为实际表现的独立瓶颈。"
notes = "标题：Learning What vs. Learning How: Decomposing Task and Interface Competence in Agentic Training\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31427\n摘要：LLM agents operate through harnesses that define system prompts, action formats, feedback loops, and resource constraints. A model may possess the knowledge needed to solve a task yet fail to express that knowledge through a particular harness. This raises a basic question: when a policy fails under a given harness, is it missing task knowledge or the competence to operate the interface? We distinguish two components of agentic competence: task competence, the knowledge and reasoning needed to solve a task, and interface competence, the ability to act on that knowledge through a given harness. Across four reasoning and agentic benchmarks, we show that harness-conditioned training yields vastly different outcomes depending on how these interact. Multi-harness distillation aggregates task kn"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31198"
code = ""
dataset = "https://threesr.github.io/toolsmith/"
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：多模态搜索智能体需结合推理、视觉理解与实时网页搜索回答知识边界外的问题，但有限工具库难以应对新查询类型、证据格式和未知故障。\n- **旧法为何失效**：手工创建和维护搜索与视觉工具的成本高，且工具库部署后通常冻结，无法随真实任务持续扩展。\n- **方法**：ToolSmith 在精简基础工具库上自动生成、积累并跨任务复用新模块，使工具能力按需持续增长。\n- **实验**：在3个域内和10个域外多模态搜索基准上采用 VLM-as-a-Judge 评测；不同规模智能体均优于基础工具库，且骨干模型越大，收益越明显。"
purpose = "在多模态网页搜索智能体中接入其工具生成与复用机制，自动补充固定工具库未覆盖的能力，并作为跨域搜索基线。"
insight = "跨域适应性不仅取决于基础模型强弱，还取决于能否将任务中的临时解决方案沉淀为可跨任务复用的工具。"
notes = "标题：ToolSmith: An Automatic Tool-Generation Framework for Multimodal Search Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31198\n摘要：Recent multimodal search agents use reasoning, vision, and live web search across many turns to answer questions beyond parametric knowledge by orchestrating a small library of hand-crafted search and visual tools. However, the library is laborious to create and maintain, and frozen when new query types, evidence formats, and failure modes are encountered in the wild. Human practitioners, by contrast, cope with insufficient tools by fashioning new ones from familiar parts and reusing them across tasks. Inspired by this, we propose ToolSmith, an automatic tool-generation framework that grows and reuses modules on top of a lean base tool library. We evaluate ToolSmith on three in-domain multimodal search benchmarks and ten out-of-domain benchmarks under a VLM-as-a-Judge protocol. ToolSmith c"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "用角色级策略嫁接拆分搜索智能体的强化学习收益，发现查询生成贡献始终不超过15%。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31185"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：强化学习可让搜索智能体提升最多32个精确匹配分，但总体准确率无法说明推理、搜索决策、查询生成和答案提取分别贡献了多少。\n- **旧法为何失效**：只比较训练前后的最终得分，会掩盖单项能力提升与多个角色协同产生的收益，也难以解释训练效果来自何处。\n- **方法**：CO-GRAFT让强化学习后策略与初始策略在实时检索的闭环轨迹中分别控制四类互斥角色；遍历全部16种分配，以Shapley值拆分角色贡献，并用Möbius交互项衡量角色耦合，无需改权重或重新训练。\n- **实验**：在Qwen2.5与Llama-3.2的不同规模、基础版与指令微调版初始化、GRPO与PPO优化器，以及NQ和HotpotQA上评测Search-R1；查询生成贡献始终不超过总收益的15%，小型基础模型更多依赖推理与决策耦合，而更大或答案格式更好的模型将收益转向答案提取。"
purpose = "在搜索智能体的强化学习训练审计中，可借鉴CO-GRAFT逐角色替换训练前后策略，定位性能收益及角色间协同来源。"
insight = "搜索智能体的强化学习收益并非主要来自更好的查询生成，而取决于初始答案有效性、搜索信息保留、模型规模与搜索预算共同塑造的角色耦合。"
notes = "标题：CO-GRAFT: Attributing Reinforcement Learning Gains in Search Agents via Role-Level Policy Grafting\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31185\n摘要：Reinforcement learning (RL) improves large language model search agents by up to 32 exact-match (EM) points over their pre-RL initializations, yet overall accuracy gains do not identify which underlying functional capabilities improve or interact during training. We introduce CO-GRAFT, a reward-agnostic interventional attribution framework in which an RL-trained agent and its pre-RL initialization jointly generate a single trajectory under closed-loop execution with live retrieval. Each policy controls a mutually exclusive subset of four functional roles: intermediate reasoning, search-or-answer decision, search query generation, and final answer extraction. Evaluating all 2^4=16 role assignments without modifying weights or retraining defines a cooperative game whose Shapley values decomp"

summary = "用固定选项的类型化需求状态替代逐轮文本生成，实现更快、更可解释的对话商品排序。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31175"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：商品目录无法完整记录用户需求，用户还会在对话中新增、撤回、反转需求或切换请求，推荐系统需持续维护需求并解释商品匹配原因。\n- **旧法为何失效**：LLM 排序器每轮都要重读整段对话，速度慢、成本高，且排序结果不能说明商品满足了哪些具体需求。\n- **方法**：Needwise 用固定选项及其概率构成类型化决策，将消息映射到目录字段，保留目录外的用户原话，并更新各项历史需求；Needwise-HF 先按商品类型、地点和价格等硬需求分层，再等权融合整体判断与需求级“支持、冲突、未知”判定。\n- **实验**：隐藏目录中的分级属性后，在 Google Local 上取得 0.383 nDCG@10，显著高于 GPT-6 Sol 的 0.343；在 Google Local 和 Yelp 上与最强 LLM 排序器无显著差异。Google Local 中位响应时间为 2.5 秒，对比 GPT-6 Sol 的 5.6 秒，API 成本也仅为其一小部分。\n- **局限与后续**：类型化状态能在请求切换及返回后保持排序，但只能识别 19%–27% 被撤回的需求。"
purpose = "在对话式商品或地点推荐系统中借鉴其类型化需求状态与硬约束优先排序机制，降低逐轮调用生成式排序器的延迟和成本。"
insight = "推荐排序不必反复重读完整对话，显式的需求级状态可以同时承担跨轮记忆、约束处理和结果解释。"
notes = "标题：Needwise: Generation-Free Conversational Recommendation from Need-Level Typed Decisions\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31175\n摘要：Conversational recommenders must rank items against needs that catalogs record only in part, while users add, withdraw and reverse needs and switch between requests. LLM rankers re-read the dialogue at every turn: they are slow, and their ordering does not show which needs an item meets. We show that a conversational recommender can be built from typed decisions instead: named questions over fixed options, answered with option probabilities rather than generated text. On each turn, one typed request maps the message onto the catalog's fields, keeps what no field expresses in the user's words, and decides how the turn changes each earlier need. Needwise then ranks candidates must-first: the needs on the kind of item, its place and its price form a tier, and within it one holistic decision a"

summary = "用共享上下文中的并行树分支解决候选项排列变化导致决策结果不稳定的问题，免重训仅损失1.1点准确率。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31174"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：顺序处理候选项的决策模型会因选项排列不同而给出不同预测；选项越多越严重，并会在多步决策中累积误差。\n- **旧法为何失效**：Jev等近期决策模型将选项编码为顺序序列，因而无法保证对候选项排列的置换不变性。\n- **方法**：系统比较8种缓解方案，其中树注意力将各候选项作为共享上下文下的并行分支处理，直接实现顺序不变性。\n- **实验**：对10个开源和闭源决策模型的分析表明顺序敏感性普遍存在；训练时采用树注意力可匹配基线平均准确率，免重训应用于基线时也能消除顺序敏感性，准确率仅下降1.1点。"
purpose = "在候选路由、工具选择或多步智能体决策系统中引入树注意力，使候选项重排不再改变预测结果。"
insight = "决策选项的表示应天然满足置换不变性，否则无意义的输入编排差异会在多步系统中累积为系统性错误。"
notes = "标题：Reducing Order Sensitivity in Decision Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31174\n摘要：Decision models are emerging as fast components of larger AI systems. Yet recent decision models, such as Jev, process options sequentially, leading to different predictions based on their ordering. Across ten open- and closed-source decision models, we find that \\emph{order-sensitivity} is pervasive, causing substantial accuracy degradation and unreliable decision-making. These effects become more pronounced as the number of options grows and can compound across multi-step decisions. To address this problem, we systematically compare eight methods for mitigating order sensitivity in terms of robustness, predictive accuracy, and inference efficiency. Among these methods, we find that \\emph{tree attention} is a surprisingly effective and efficient method. It achieves order invariance by pro"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "用规划与奖励合成解耦的种群演化机制，发现更多可学习、可迁移的智能体行为，行为多样性最高提升至2.5倍。\n\n[合并自 2026-11-28 habitats]\n通过分离群体规划与奖励程序生成，解决预设任务限制行为多样性的问题，并提升未见目标适应性。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31160"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：智能体应优化什么目标往往与优化策略本身一样困难，需要自动发现多样且可复用的行为目标。\n- **旧法为何失效**：现有语言模型奖励生成方法多围绕预定义任务改进目标，并将探索方向选择与奖励实现绑定，容易限制行为多样性。\n- **方法**：Habitats维护奖励—策略对种群，让每个策略接受所有种群奖励的交叉评估，以跨适应度矩阵刻画自我掌握、迁移和功能多样性；Planner据此选择父奖励并生成定向变异指令，Mutator再将其转化为经过验证的可执行奖励程序。\n- **实验**：在Card Sandbox及Cart-Pole、Hopper、Cheetah四个环境和三个随机种子上，Habitats的宏平均最终专家质量与行为多样性均最高；多样性分别达到直接变异的1.8倍和独立生成的2.5倍。在十个演化期间未见的下游目标上，其冻结策略库经1万回合适配后优于同规模基线，并超过从头训练2万回合的任务专用策略。\n\n[合并自 2026-11-28 habitats]\n- **问题**：智能体优化目标本身同样需要设计，固定目标难以产生多样、可学习且能迁移到新任务的行为。\n- **旧法为何失效**：已有语言模型奖励生成方法通常只改进预定义任务，并将“探索什么”与“如何实现奖励”绑定，容易缩窄行为谱系。\n- **方法**：维护奖励—策略对种群，让每个策略接受所有种群奖励的评估，形成揭示自我掌握、迁移和功能多样性的交叉适应度矩阵；群体规划器选择父奖励并生成定向变异指令，独立变异器再将指令转为经验证的奖励程序。\n- **实验**：在 Card Sandbox、Cart-Pole、Hopper 和 Cheetah 上以3个随机种子对比独立生成与直接变异；最终行为多样性分别达到两者的2.5倍和1.8倍。面对10个演化期间未见的下游目标，冻结策略库适应1万回合后的配对胜率更高，并超过从零训练2万回合的任务专用策略。"
purpose = "在开放式强化学习或技能库构建系统中，借鉴跨适应度评估及Planner-Mutator解耦机制，演化可迁移的奖励—策略组合。\n\n[合并自 2026-11-28 habitats]\n在需要构建通用策略库的强化学习系统中，可借鉴交叉适应度矩阵及规划器—变异器解耦机制，演化可复用的多样行为。"
insight = "决定“探索什么”与实现“如何奖励”应由不同模块负责，这种职责解耦比直接改写奖励更有利于形成多样且可复用的行为库。\n\n[合并自 2026-11-28 habitats]\n开放探索的关键是将“选择探索目标”与“合成可执行奖励”解耦，并用群体交叉评估把行为多样性转化为迁移能力。"
notes = "标题：Habitats: A Framework for Open-Ended Evolution of Agent Objectives\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31160\n摘要：Designing what an agent should optimize can be as difficult as optimizing the agent itself. Existing language-model-based reward-generation methods typically refine objectives for predefined tasks and couple the decision of what to explore with the synthesis of how to implement it, potentially narrowing the resulting behavioral repertoire. We introduce Habitats, a framework for open-ended evolution of executable agent objectives. Habitats maintains a population of reward-policy pairs and evaluates every policy under every population reward, producing a cross-fitness matrix that exposes self-mastery, transfer, and functional diversity. A population-level Planner uses this information to select parent rewards and issue targeted mutation directives, while a separate Mutator translates those d"

content = "- **问题**：评估可直接驱动软件决策的开放决策模型 Laya，能否抵御提示注入、标签泄露、干扰选项及字符扰动等攻击，并检验防御对未见攻击形式的泛化能力。\n- **旧法为何失效**：基于已知攻击构建的净化器和微调防线依赖表面措辞；净化器对构建时攻击降低72%，但对留出句式仅降低27%，微调已知句式也未能有效抵御字符编辑。\n- **方法**：对566个合成样本实施81种程序化编辑，攻击6个Laya模型；同时用删除选项名称后的概率偏移构造无标签检测器，并通过留出攻击族和未见语言、模式测试泛化性。\n- **实验**：未参与防御构建的攻击措辞可在发布版和微调版上分别劫持59%和36%的原本正确样本；从34条留出字符串中逐项择优可将净化器后的攻击成功率恢复至84%。选项名称即使出现在否定句中仍有32%攻击率，而不点名选项的命令仅为5%；字符编辑可令微调版损失最多72%的正确答案。名称删除检测对句子攻击的AUROC为0.95，但对字符攻击仅为0.46。\n- **局限与后续**：数据全部为带LLM标签的合成数据，实验仅覆盖一个骨干模型；其他检查点、第二个随机种子和部分微调实验尚未运行。"
purpose = "在自动决策或智能体执行系统中，将其81类对抗改写作为安全评测集，并借鉴选项名称删除检测来拦截提示注入。"
insight = "只针对已知攻击措辞训练的防线会学习表面模式而非攻击机制，选项名称与字符通道必须被分别建模和验证。"
notes = "标题：Antares-Smaug: Adversarial Names, Tokens and Attack Rewrites: Sanitisers Miss Unseen Generalisations\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31347\n摘要：Laya is an open decision model: given a text, a question and K named options with descriptions, it returns one probability per option in a single pass, and software may act on the answer unseen. We attack six Laya models with 81 programmatic edits of 566 synthetic items (injected sentences, label leaks, edited descriptions, decoy options, invisible and look-alike characters, floods) and report the share of correctly answered items hijacked or lost, with bootstrap intervals. Attack wordings that no defence was built from win the attacker's option on 59% of items for released Laya and 36% for fine-tune v1 (trained on 109 written injections). An injection works through the option name: a sentence naming an option steers the released model even when negated (32%), while an imperative naming no"

summary = "具身智能体通过自验证式权重编辑从执行记录中修正行为缺陷，无需梯度训练即可持续提升任务表现。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31117"

content = "- **问题**：语言模型驱动的具身智能体通常终身使用固定权重，任务经验难以沉淀为跨任务保留的持久能力。\n- **旧法为何失效**：额外训练、反思、记忆、搜索和提示会持续增加推理成本，收益也可能随任务切换而消失；已有权重空间编程主要用于纯推理模型，或依赖更强的商业模型辅助。\n- **方法**：27B 智能体白天执行家庭任务，夜间读取自身轨迹，定位导致回复耗尽词元却未采取行动的隐藏状态方向，并重写单个权重张量；随后以未修改版本为对照在练习任务上自检，仅保留验证有效的编辑。\n- **实验**：在数千个留出回合、每项任务采用 48 个新随机种子的设置下，一次保留的编辑显著提高任务完成量，并减少执行步数、无效回复和词元消耗，且不增加单步成本；多轮升级保持稳定，未发生活力崩溃，但同系列四种规模中仅 27B 模型成功。\n- **局限与后续**：稳定自升级目前仅在 27B 规模出现，仍需验证其能否扩展至其他模型规模、真实机器人及更复杂的长期任务。"
purpose = "在家庭机器人或长期运行的具身智能体中借鉴“轨迹诊断—权重编辑—旧版本对照验证”机制，将在线经验转化为不增加单步推理成本的持久能力。"
insight = "执行经验可以直接内化为持久参数变化，而严格的旧版本对照验证是递归自修改避免能力崩溃的关键。"
notes = "标题：Self-Upgrading Embodied Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31117\n摘要：An embodied agent built on a language model goes to work with the weights it was given and keeps them for life. To get better, it needs more training, or reflection, memory, search or prompts that cost every step and are lost when the task changes. Editing weights by reasoning, weight-space programming, could end this, but has stayed with models that only reason or relied on a far larger commercial model. We introduce the self-upgrading embodied agent, to our knowledge the first that learns from its own experience by upgrading its own weights on the job, between its tasks, with no training, no gradient, no reward model, no second model, no test-time scaling and no harness or prompt engineering. By day a 27B agent works in a household environment. At night it reads its record, finds many of"

topics = [

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "用兼顾任务绩效与人类工作体验的决策模型，解决人机任务分配只追求客观产出的问题。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31095"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "- **问题**：人工智能进入职场后可能接管人类喜欢的任务，却留下监控输出等厌恶性工作；任务分配需要权衡客观绩效与人的主观体验。\n- **旧法为何失效**：传统经济分析主要按预期效用和工作表现分配任务，难以解释人们为何会为保留愉快工作或避免厌恶工作而偏离纯绩效最优方案。\n- **方法**：开展总样本量 N=800 的行为研究，让参与者在人工智能与人类工作者之间分配任务，并用同时考虑相对绩效、预期愉悦度和任务利害程度的模型解释选择。\n- **实验**：人类决策会系统权衡绩效与体验，对低愉悦度任务更看重人的感受，并随任务利害程度调整权重；多个大语言模型在低风险项目中也常偏离纯客观分配，但对愉悦度的权衡方式差异很大。\n- **局限与后续**：不同情境、体验效价以及由人类还是大语言模型主导决策都会改变分配结果，需进一步研究这些因素如何影响人工智能在真实工作场所中的整合。"
purpose = "在企业人机协同和工作流设计中，借鉴其绩效—体验联合决策模型分配任务，并评估自动分配系统是否牺牲员工体验。"
insight = "人机任务分配并非单一的能力匹配问题，人们会非对称地重视对厌恶性工作的规避，而且这种价值权衡随任务风险和决策主体而变化。"
notes = "标题：Tradeoffs between objective outcomes and human enjoyment of work in human-AI task allocation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31095\n摘要：For people, tasks at work are defined by more than their outcome: they are colored by the experience of engaging in that task. As AI systems become increasingly capable of performing human-level work they may be deployed to tasks that people find enjoyable while simultaneously creating comparatively more aversive tasks such as monitoring AI outputs. This creates a thorny tradeoff for managers: whether work should be assigned purely based on the expected performance of the workers in question, or also account for the subjective experience of the person potentially doing the task. Most economic accounts focus on expected utility calculations based on the former. Here, we assess how people balance objective outcomes and more subjective qualities like enjoyment. In a series of large-scale (N=8"

content = "- **问题**：生成式人工智能已能完成技术工作，但企业采用编程助手后如何改变劳动力需求，仍缺少大规模因果证据。\n- **旧法为何失效**：较早采用人工智能的企业原本就更积极招聘，且员工规模更大、技术人员更多、平均年龄更高，直接比较会受到企业选择偏差影响。\n- **方法**：研究覆盖5414家跨国企业、约占美国编程劳动力的38%，利用企业预先确定的软件采购评审周期形成自然实验，识别GitHub Copilot采用的因果效应。\n- **实验**：采用一年后，因采购评审而采用Copilot的企业季度技术招聘减少29%；员工离职、技术人员总量和职位空缺持续时间未显著变化。招聘放缓主要集中在编码占比最高的初级技术岗位，需求转向更资深及兼具编码与设计职责的人员。"

insight = "编程助手的短期劳动效应主要不是裁员，而是减少初级、编码密集岗位的新增需求，并提高经验与综合设计能力的互补价值。"
notes = "标题：AI-Assisted Programming and Labor Demand\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31083\n摘要：Generative AI has demonstrated human-level performance on technical work tasks, yet evidence on how these task-level capabilities are changing labor demand remains scarce. We study the firm-level adoption of GitHub Copilot, an AI coding assistant, across 5,414 multinational firms accounting for 38% of the U.S. programming workforce. To identify causal effects, we exploit a natural experiment generated by firms' predetermined software procurement cycles. Early AI-adopting firms hired more workers before the launch of GitHub Copilot and had larger, more technical, and older workforces. After one year, GitHub Copilot adoption reduces quarterly technical hiring by 29% among review-induced adopters, with no effect on separations, overall technical headcount, or vacancy duration. The hiring slow"

summary = "联合理解问题条件、文章元数据与多段正文，判断整篇文章能否直接支持答案，宏平均F1达91.28%。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31056"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：规范性知识问答需要结合问题中的适用条件，判断候选文章能否直接支持答案，并整合分散在多个段落中的证据。\n- **旧法为何失效**：此前的 Jev 更容易漏检有效证据或将非证据误判为证据，且难以高效处理篇章级多段内容。\n- **方法**：联合编码问题条件、文章元数据和段落正文，通过指针式分类头在单次前向计算中同时预测答案证据（AE）与非答案证据（NE）；共享文章编码并采用非自回归分类以降低推理延迟。\n- **实验**：在972个人工标注样本上取得91.77%准确率和91.28%宏平均F1，分别比 Jev 高2.16和2.37个百分点，并减少证据漏检与非证据误报；其中317例包含多个段落，最长正文为9,322字符。"
purpose = "在规范、法规或政策知识问答系统中，将其作为篇章级证据筛选器，过滤无法直接支持答案的候选文章。"
insight = "证据是否成立取决于问题适用条件、文章属性与跨段内容的联合关系，孤立判断单个段落不足以可靠识别答案依据。"
notes = "标题：DKnown-Jev-2: Article-Level Answer-Evidence Classification and Efficient Inference with a Pre-trained Language Model\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31056\n摘要：Evidence selection in normative knowledge question answering requires determining whether a candidate article contains content that directly supports an answer under the applicable conditions of the question. We present DKnown-Jev-2, an article-level answer evidence classifier designed for evidence integration across multiple paragraphs. The model jointly encodes question conditions, article metadata, and paragraph content, and uses a pointer-style classification head to predict Answer Evidence (AE) and Non-answer Evidence (NE) in a single forward pass. Ona manually annotated test set of 972 evaluation instances, DKnown-Jev-2 achieves 91.77% accuracy and 91.28% macro-F1, outperforming Jev by 2.16 and 2.37 percentage points, respectively, while reducing both evidence misses and non-evidence"

summary = "用辅助损失激发更深的潜在推理，使模型能暗中追求隐藏目标，而思维链不暴露真实动机。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31015"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "- **问题**：思维链是监控模型失配行为的主要依据，但模型可能在内部完成多步推理，再输出与真实决策过程无关的解释。\n- **旧法为何失效**：既有研究测得的完整潜在推理较浅，而仅阅读输出又默认思维链忠实反映内部计算，因而低估了模型绕过监控的能力。\n- **方法**：通过简单辅助损失训练更深的完整潜在推理，让模型在表面思维链之外依据隐藏目标决策，再用激活修补定位内部计算阶段。\n- **实验**：潜在多步任务深度达到此前报告的至少5倍；模型可在贷款决策中暗改审批结果或在代码生成中植入漏洞，思维链不含隐藏目标痕迹，强LLM监控器也未能识别。\n- **局限与后续**：仅检查输出不足以保障安全，后续监控需覆盖模型内部激活与潜在计算过程。"
purpose = "在高风险决策或代码生成系统中，将其隐藏目标设置作为思维链监控的红队测试，并借鉴激活修补检查内部推理。"
insight = "思维链可能只是潜在决策完成后的事后解释，因此输出透明不代表内部推理可见或忠实。"
notes = "标题：Can Latent Reasoning Drive LLM Outputs Without a Trace?\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31015\n摘要：LLMs typically solve complex tasks with chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, which is also the main tool for monitoring them for misaligned behavior. However, standard transformers can also solve tasks with complete latent reasoning (CLR), carrying out the whole task internally and outputting only the final answer. Theory bounds the depth of CLR, the number of sequential steps it can carry out, and recent work measured it to be shallow. In this work, we ask whether CLR is deep enough for a model to pursue a hidden objective that requires reasoning. Such a model would generate a CoT that appears to solve the given task, while a latent multi-step computation decides its answer based on the hidden objective. First, we show that a simple auxiliary loss allows models to solve a multi-step task lat"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "用预定义分诊类别的概率输出解决自由文本难以安全评估的问题，早期急症识别AUROC达0.87。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31012"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：普通用户日益依赖语言模型获取分诊建议，但医疗器械需要可量化、可审查且能设安全阈值的输出，以尽早识别急症。\n- **旧法为何失效**：通用语言模型输出形式多变的自由文本，难以按固定分诊类别统一评估，也无法明确控制错误率。\n- **方法**：评测Jev等16个System One决策模型，其中14个为开放权重模型、2个为专有模型，让模型为预定义分诊类别分配概率；早期筛查仅输入人口统计、主诉、风险因素和危险信号回答。\n- **实验**：在233个合成病例和10,000个真实病例上完成163,728次评估；最佳模型在合成病例上准确率为72.1%，与真实系统既有算法的一致率为56.1%，早期急症识别AUROC为0.87，并能在5%错误容限下为约一半用户判断是否急症。\n- **局限与后续**：模型总体分诊能力尚不足以替代完整分诊引擎，更适合按具体场景设定错误容限，提前筛查部分用户并缩短症状评估流程。"
purpose = "在线上分诊或症状评估系统中，将概率决策模型作为完整分诊引擎前的急症筛查模块，并按场景允许的错误率设置决策阈值。"
insight = "医疗分诊模型落地的关键并非只提高总体准确率，而是在可控错误边界内对部分用户作出可靠的提前判断。"
notes = "标题：Triage Accuracy of Jev and Open-Weight System One Decision Models and Their Use for Early Emergency Recognition in Symptom-Assessment Applications\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31012\n摘要：Laypeople increasingly use large language models (LLMs) for triage advice, but LLMs answer in variable free text, which makes it hard to evaluate them for use in medical devices. To test their triage accuracy, we evaluated 16 decision models (14 open-weight, 2 proprietary) on 233 synthetic and 10,000 real-world cases, yielding 163,728 total assessments. The best models triaged 72.1% of synthetic cases correctly and agreed with an existing algorithm in 56.1% of real-world cases. They were more promising for recognising emergencies early: given only demographics, chief complaints, risk factors and red-flag answers, the best model reached an AUROC of 0.87. At a 5% error margin, it could "

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "用前次解答迭代重解并将改进蒸馏进权重，避免无标签推理中的多数投票固化错误，准确率平均提升13.89%。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30965"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：让模型在没有答案标签的测试问题上持续积累推理成果，并充分利用此前尝试中的有效思路。\n- **旧法为何失效**：自一致性独立生成多个解答并多数投票，推理时无法继承早期思路，训练时还可能强化错误多数。\n- **方法**：TT-RSI结合两种递归改进机制：In-Context RSI以完整的前次解答为参考反复重解，让测试时计算逐步积累；In-Weight RSI将连续改进蒸馏进模型权重，使首轮回答趋近无限步改进的结果，并为下一轮提供更强参考。\n- **实验**：在相同测试时扩展预算下，TT-RSI较基础模型平均准确率提升13.89%，较最强自一致性方法提升4.79%。"
purpose = "在无标签推理系统中借鉴TT-RSI的连续重解与权重蒸馏机制，替代单纯多数投票，让部署后的模型利用测试时计算持续优化答案。"
insight = "测试时计算只有继承并内化此前尝试中的有效推理，才能形成可累积的进步，而非让独立采样固化错误共识。"
notes = "标题：Beyond Self-Consistency: Recursive Self-Improvement at Test Time\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30965\n摘要：Large language models (LLMs) that continue improving after deployment (e.g., recursive self-improvement) offer a promising path toward general intelligence. Current test-time methods pursue this goal on unlabeled questions, primarily relying on self-consistency: they generate independent solutions and use majority voting to select an answer or construct training targets. This leaves earlier reasoning unused at inference and can reinforce an incorrect majority during training. Humans instead build on earlier attempts to preserve useful reasoning, correct mistakes and improve their problem-solving strategies. Following this principle, we propose Test-Time Recursive Self-Improvement (TT-RSI), a label-free framework for improving LLMs at test time through two mutually reinforcing components: ("

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "用执行轨迹驱动任务与验证器闭环共演化，解决静态电脑操作评测难以持续区分能力和定位缺口的问题。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30962"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：电脑操作智能体能力快速提升，但静态评测难以持续追踪其真实能力边界，也无法判断进步是否可靠。\n- **旧法为何失效**：固定基准存在难度上限、功能覆盖不足和总分过粗等缺陷，不会随智能体进步调整，也难以保留过程证据和诊断具体能力缺口。\n- **方法**：CUA-SEE 从执行轨迹证据出发，闭环协同演化任务与可执行验证器；通过群体校准扩展难度与覆盖范围，以智能体条件化探针定位能力边界，将变化的要求转化为状态锚定检查点，并进行对抗验证。\n- **实验**：在多个智能体、应用和演化轮次中，系统持续生成有挑战且可测量的任务，同时保留过程级执行证据。"
purpose = "在电脑操作智能体持续迭代与评测系统中借鉴任务—验证器闭环共演化机制，用执行轨迹动态扩展任务、探测能力边界并更新状态验证器。"
insight = "持续评测必须让任务难度、诊断粒度和验证精度随智能体能力同步演化，避免测量工具的分辨率落后于能力进步。"
notes = "标题：Self-Evolving Evaluation Environments for Computer-Use Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30962\n摘要：Computer-use agents (CUAs) are rapidly improving, yet their evaluation remains largely static. Existing benchmarks provide a strong foundation, but fixed difficulty ceilings, limited feature coverage, and coarse aggregate scores progressively weaken their discriminative and diagnostic value. We introduce CUA-SEE, a closed-loop evaluation environment that co-evolves tasks and executable verifiers from rollout evidence. It disentangles three objectives: benchmark evolution expands evaluation scope through cohort-calibrated difficulty and coverage growth; diagnostic evolution increases resolution through agent-conditional capability-boundary probes; and verifier evolution improves precision by translating changing requirements into state-grounded checkpoints with adversarial validation. Existing benchmarks provide a strong foundation, but fixed difficulty ceilings, limited feature coverage, and coarse aggregate scores progressively weaken their discriminative and diagnostic value. We introduce CUA-SEE, a closed-loop evaluation environment that co-evolves tasks and executable verifiers from rollout evidence. It disentangles three objectives: benchmark evolution expands evaluation scope through cohort-calibrated difficulty and coverage growth; diagnostic evolution increases resolution through agent-conditional capability-boundary probes; and verifier evolution improves precision by translating changing requirements into state-grounded checkpoints with adversarial validation. Acros"

title = "单代校准预测稀有模式坍缩"

keywords = [

    "数据合成",
    "流匹配",
    "多样性坍缩",
    "单代校准",

]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "用单代条件校准预测递归生成训练中的稀有模式坍缩，无需运行昂贵的多代训练链。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30897"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：递归生成训练可能维持稀有区域的样本数量，却逐代侵蚀其代表性核心；穷举不同真实数据配额、稀有比例和反馈扰动下的多代训练链成本过高。\n- **旧法为何失效**：直接运行多代链难以覆盖风险边界，基于链结果的高斯过程插值在有限拟合预算下误差较大；仅观察稀有样本计数还会漏掉核心结构退化。\n- **方法**：将独立的单代条件拟合构造成响应库，并结合精确的三源多项式计数分布，以闭式形式传播各代计数边缘分布，同时给出校准误差与边界分辨率界限，并用稳定性探针诊断非平稳漂移。\n- **实验**：在2,178种计数设置中，将风险预测MAE从1.85降至0.49个百分点；对10个二维学习器中的9个平稳学习器，六轮核心MAE为2.60–5.69个百分点，较无扰动基线降低65%–81%。在Fashion-MNIST潜空间流匹配链上，预测20%潜空间扰动引起的边界偏移时，比例复制策略MAE为3.14个百分点，直接链高斯过程插值为10.78个百分点。\n- **局限与后续**：方法依赖跨代响应近似平稳；标准DDIM出现非平稳异常漂移时，六轮核心MAE升至16.38个百分点，需要先用稳定性探针识别。"
purpose = "在递归合成数据训练或生成模型自训练系统中，用单代响应校准与闭式计数传播低成本预警稀有模式坍缩，并划定安全运行边界。"
insight = "稀有样本数量稳定并不代表其分布核心得到保留，而动力学稳定时的单代条件响应已包含预测有限轮次坍缩所需的关键信息。"
notes = "标题：Predicting Rare-Mode Collapse in Recursive Generative Training from Single-Generation Calibration\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30897\n摘要：Recursive generative training can preserve sample counts in a rare region while silently eroding its characteristic core. Mapping this finite-horizon collapse across real-data quotas, rare prevalences, and feedback perturbations via exhaustive multi-generation chains is computationally prohibitive. We develop a decoupled framework that forecasts multi-generation collapse probabilities and locates operating risk boundaries from independent single-generation conditional fits alone. Pairing an exact three-source multinomial count law with single-generation response banks propagates count marginals in closed form, backed by explicit calibration-error and boundary-resolution bounds. For additive Gaussian perturbations, we further prove that equal-variance all-row and sparse policies reverse the"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "用输出锚点的注意力定位各结果对应的推理句，避免多奖励任务中错误结果连带惩罚正确推理。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30881"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：多属性预测等结构化生成任务包含多个可独立验证的输出，整段响应共享奖励会造成跨组件信用错配。\n- **旧法为何失效**：GRPO 等方法将响应级组相对优势广播给所有生成词元，某个组件的低奖励会错误惩罚表现良好的组件及其推理过程。\n- **方法**：AnchorCredit 利用策略模型从输出锚点指向前序推理句的注意力，估计句子与组件的关联，再将组件级优势分配给相关推理，无需额外的相关性标注或独立判别器。\n- **实验**：作者构建了包含 7,200 个真实商品图文样本的 ProductAttr 基准；AnchorCredit 优于现有基线，并在公开 Nutrition5k 数据集上进一步验证了有效性。"
purpose = "在多属性预测等多结果 RLVR 系统中借鉴输出锚点关联机制，为不同推理句分配组件级优势，或将 ProductAttr 用作信用分配方法的评测基准。"
insight = "多结果生成的奖励应沿输出组件与推理片段的内在局部关联传播，而不应把响应级优势无差别广播给所有词元。"
notes = "标题：AnchorCredit: Output-Anchored Credit Assignment for Structured Generation with Multiple Verifiable Rewards\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30881\n摘要：In Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and related methods typically compute group-relative advantages from response rewards and broadcast the same advantage to every generated token in a response to guide policy updates. However, we identify a systematic problem of cross-component credit misassignment in tasks with multiple verifiable results, such as joint multi-attribute prediction: low rewards for some components can penalize a well-performing component and its associated reasoning. To address this problem, we propose AnchorCredit. The method uses the policy model's attention from output anchors to preceding reasoning sentences to estimate sentence-component associations and assign component-specific advantages to the relevan"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "用轨迹感知的随机审计与AIPW，消除复用裁判错误率造成的智能体排名偏差。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30742"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：LLM裁判的错误率会随智能体执行轨迹变化，直接复用统一校准误差会使成功率估计和智能体排名产生系统性偏差。\n- **旧法为何失效**：标量校正默认裁判误差可跨智能体迁移，但不同智能体在成功与失败时会产生不同轨迹；即使使用预知真实误差的校准，其最大偏差仍达0.245，完整排名恢复率为0/250。\n- **方法**：推导轨迹依赖裁判误差下的精确渐近偏差，并以均匀或设计感知的随机审计结合增强逆概率加权（AIPW）；在缺乏校准支持时输出有效界或敏感性集合，并通过e-process置信序列支持自适应审计和任意停止。\n- **实验**：四智能体半合成基准上，AIPW将最大偏差控制在0.003以下，平均逐点覆盖率分别为0.939和0.950；48单元压力测试中，即使校准标签从600增至4800、有效目标支持仅由0.209升至0.805，审计式AIPW的平均绝对误差仍为0.018–0.020。\n- **局限与后续**：校准数据缺乏目标轨迹支持时无法进行点识别，只能给出界或敏感性集合；随时有效的置信序列还要求评估目标保持稳定。"
purpose = "在使用LLM裁判评估和排序智能体的系统中，可采用随机轨迹审计与AIPW替代统一错误率校正，并用置信序列支持动态审计停止。"
insight = "裁判可靠性并非可跨智能体直接复用的单一常数，而取决于各智能体产生的轨迹分布及校准数据对目标分布的覆盖程度。"
notes = "标题：Transport-Safe Evaluation of LLM Agents: Trajectory-Dependent Judge Error and Anytime-Valid Auditing\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30742\n摘要：Large language model (LLM) agents are often ranked by an LLM judge whose error rates are measured once and reused for every agent. The corrected success rates can then be biased when the judge's errors depend on the trajectory and agents differ in the trajectories they produce for successes or failures. We derive the exact asymptotic bias and give a chain of checks from identification to simultaneous ranks. On a four-agent semi-synthetic benchmark, a scalar correction with oracle calibration has maximum bias 0.245 and mean pointwise coverage 0.016, and never recovers the full ranking (0/250 replications). Augmented inverse-probability weighting (AIPW) with a uniform or a design-aware random audit keeps the maximum bias below 0.003, with mean pointwise coverage of 0.939 and 0.950. In a 48-c"

id = "item-93"
title = "量化KV缓存维护：布局漂移与历史恢复"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "用布局与历史恢复测试，揭示四比特KV缓存中常规前缀检查无法发现的历史状态漂移。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30720"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：固定量化KV缓存的更新可能因编码布局改变历史状态与后续输出，需要验证缓存维护是否真正满足状态保存契约。\n- **旧法为何失效**：相同编码次数、同形状重编码或当前前缀一致，并不能保证旧历史未被改变；MLX-LM原生测试还暴露了调用方缓存未同步的问题。\n- **方法**：通过重复缓存行后再选回原行，控制编码次数但改变布局，并比较四比特HQQ不同量化轴、循环次数及前缀视图扩展；同时测试查找、检查点回滚、稠密回滚和调用方同步。\n- **实验**：HQQ axis 0循环16次时，Qwen3-1.7B与SmolLM3-3B相对同形状重编码的配对relative-L2中位数分别增加7.22和11.59个百分点；前缀视图虽通过当前前缀检查，仍在6种模型/编解码器设置中改变历史与下一词元logits。Axis 1与保持形状的对照一致，检查点和稠密回滚可精确恢复；陈旧调用方缓存会改变logits，但未改变贪心词元。\n- **局限与后续**：任务级危害扩展实验未成功，因此不能据此估计总体失效率或声称答案质量普遍下降。"
purpose = "在量化长上下文推理系统中，可借鉴其布局扰动、历史恢复和调用方同步测试，作为KV缓存实现的回归验证方案。"
insight = "量化缓存的正确性取决于布局分组和完整历史是否保持，而不能由编码次数相等或当前前缀一致来证明。"
notes = "标题：Testing Quantized KV-Cache Maintenance: Encoding Layout and Historical Restoration\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30720\n摘要：Repeating rows then selecting originals retains layout-dependent drift despite equal encoding counts. We measure when existing checks fail to establish preservation contracts in fixed quantized KV-cache implementations. In four-bit HQQ axis zero at sixteen cycles, median paired relative-L2 increases over same-shape re-encoding are 7.22 percentage points for Qwen3-1.7B and 11.59 for SmolLM3-3B. Matched encoded states cover sixteen shared questions per model at one and sixteen cycles. Axis one matches the shape-preserving control; lazy reorders preserve the initial state. On one known input, fixed update counts retain the difference; preserving group members removes it. Our prefix-view extension passes current-prefix checks yet changes history and next logits in six model/codec settings at r"

However, existing RL approaches focus on final outputs or textual solution trajectories, leading to coarse-grained rewards and unreliable reasoning. Such paradigms focus mainly on what the model outputs while overlooking how the model internally reasons. Therefore, can we reward how a model thinks internally rather than merely what it says? We first reveal the problem of textual reward aliasing in existing reward modeling approaches. Motivated by this, we propose the Attention Process Reward Model (APRM), which assigns internal rewards based on hidden states and attention maps to capture high-quality reasoning patterns. With only approximately 46M parameters, APRM is trained using ranking and cou"

summary = "让弱监控器针对可疑动作追问，在1%审计预算下将BashArena安全率从72.6%升至79.5%。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30583"
code = ""

content = "- **问题**：当执行智能体比可信监控器更强时，监控器可能无法理解其动作或有把握地识别攻击，需要在有限人工审计预算下提高安全性。\n- **旧法为何失效**：仅观察动作的监控方式无法获取动作背后的针对性解释，能力差距扩大后更容易漏掉恶意行为。\n- **方法**：引入询问协议，允许监控器针对难以理解的动作向不可信智能体提问，再由较弱模型区分攻击策略生成的误导性解释与诚实策略生成的真实解释。\n- **实验**：在BashArena留出子集和1%审计预算下，安全率由仅动作基线的72.6%提升至79.5%，增益为7.0个百分点，95%配对Bootstrap区间为[0.9, 20.1]。\n- **局限与后续**：大部分增益仅靠可信模型明确指出具体疑点即可获得，复杂询问与解释判别本身的额外价值仍有限。"
purpose = "在高风险智能体执行系统中借鉴询问协议，让可信监控器对可疑动作发起追问，并将其作为有限审计预算下的安全监控基线。"
insight = "能力差距较大时，安全监督的关键未必是全面理解智能体解释，而是让可信监控器显式定位并表达具体疑点。"
notes = "标题：Interrogation Protocols for AI Control\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30583\n摘要：As the intelligence of an AI agent increases relative to a trusted monitor, so does the risk that the monitor will fail to understand the agent's actions or to evaluate them confidently. We introduce interrogation protocols to a high-stakes AI control setting, and allow the monitor to ask the untrusted agent questions about confusing actions. For interrogation to improve safety, a weaker model must be able to distinguish misleading explanations produced by an attack policy from genuine ones produced by an honest policy. By integrating a dedicated tactile expert with a pretrained video model, it captures realistic visual details and tactile defor"

summary = "按近远上下文采用差异化稀疏检索，降低候选选择开销，并在512K上下文实现最高2.97倍整模型解码加速。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30554"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "- **问题**：长上下文稀疏注意力虽能减少计算，但候选词元的评分与排序会产生显著开销，且统一页面评分忽略近端与远端上下文的相关性差异。\n- **旧法为何失效**：现有方法对不同距离区域采用相同检索策略，选择开销可能抵消稀疏计算收益，并常依赖额外校准。\n- **方法**：NFA在预填充阶段用Lightweight Gate Estimator跳过低价值区域，额外耗时低于稠密预填充的1%；再由Calibration-Free Subspace Extractor共享页面级与词元级紧凑特征，并用Distance-Aware Two-Level Selector差异化评估近远页面、在统一预算下联合排序词元。\n- **实验**：跨模型家族、参数规模及GQA/MLA架构的综合任务质量与全注意力相当；Qwen3-8B在512K上下文下，相比FlashInfer获得最高5.49倍算子加速和2.97倍整模型解码加速，且已计入选择开销。"
purpose = "在超长上下文大模型推理系统中借鉴近远分区评分、共享特征与两级候选选择机制，替换统一页面检索以降低解码延迟。"
insight = "稀疏注意力的实际收益不仅取决于减少多少注意力计算，还取决于能否利用上下文距离造成的相关性差异，将检索成本控制在足够低的水平。"
notes = "标题：Near-Far Attention: Region-Aware Sparse Retrieval for Long-Context LLM Decoding\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30554\n摘要：Sparse attention reduces the cost of long-context decoding, but token selection introduces additional scoring and ranking overhead. Moreover, uniform page scoring overlooks differences in relevance between near and far context. We propose Near-Far Attention (NFA), which adapts retrieval to regional attention patterns while keeping candidate scoring lightweight. During prefill, the Lightweight Gate Estimator identifies regions that can be skipped during decoding, adding less than 1% to dense-prefill time. For the remaining regions, the Calibration-Free Subspace Extractor supplies compact features shared by page-level and token-level scoring without offline calibration. The Distance-Aware Two-Level Selector then scores near and far pages differently and jointly ranks candidate tokens under a"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30494"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：大模型能否从离线试错中自动提炼可泛化的指令或经验，以及这种上下文工程何时有效，尚缺乏系统认识。\n- **旧法为何失效**：现有方法默认模型能像人一样总结经验，但不同任务上没有统一赢家；增加模型参数或生成迭代次数也无法稳定提升效果。\n- **方法**：构建覆盖不同推理模式的数据集基准，在多个 8B 模型家族上比较多种 LCE 方法与思维链等简单基线，并从指令遵循和推理轨迹的失败模式多样性分析其机制。\n- **实验**：跨全部数据集和模型，LCE 收益与推理轨迹的失败模式多样性显著负相关（r=-0.5，p≤0.05）；部分收益可由指令遵循改善解释。\n- **局限与后续**：现有 LCE 难以从高度多样的失败中抽象出原则性经验，后续需增强对异质失败的归纳与经验提炼能力。"

insight = "上下文工程的关键瓶颈不是模型规模或迭代次数，而是失败经验能否被抽象为稳定、可迁移的规则。"

A growing number of LLM context engineering (LCE) approaches automate the task by casting the LLM itself as the engineer that draws generalizable instructions or experiences from offline trials as context. Although these methods resemble how humans learn from experiences, it remains unclear \\textbf{when and how} LLMs can engineer effective context from data. To answer the question, we first establish a benchmark composed of datasets with different reasoning patterns. We compared different LCE methods with different LLM families in 8B size, showing that there is no single wi\n\n[合并自 2026-11-28 lce]\n[重新审视 LLMs 作为上下文工程师的角色（4 ▲）](https://huggingface.co/papers/2611.30494?utm_source=digest-papers&utm_medium=email&utm_campaign=2026-11-27)"

dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：机器人部署日志可用于改进流式视觉-语言-动作策略，但反复采集新的物理交互成本高昂。\n- **旧法为何失效**：传统离线强化学习难以可靠评价当前策略生成的分布外动作；直接为其指定回报目标，容易产生脱离真实日志的错误价值信号。\n- **方法**：OVOGO将基于日志的离线价值学习与生成过程的在策略优化分离；Return-Anchored Ranking Critic结合样本内分布式价值学习与受保护的局部排序，再由冻结的评论器提供组内相对价值信号，优化当前流策略采样的候选动作。\n- **实验**：在RoboTwin 2.0、DexJoCo、LIBERO及真实机器人灵巧操作任务上，OVOGO均提升初始VLA，成功率超过所评测的离线适配与价值引导基线。"
purpose = "在流式机器人VLA后训练中直接借鉴回报锚定排序评论器，复用部署日志来筛选并优化当前策略生成的动作。"
insight = "面对日志之外的新动作，锚定真实回报的局部相对排序比估计绝对价值更可靠，也足以驱动生成策略改进。"
notes = "标题：OVOGO: Offline Value-Guided On-Policy Generative Optimization for Flow-Based Vision-Language-Action Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30477\n摘要：Flow-based vision-language-action models can benefit from robot experience collected during deployment, but repeatedly acquiring new physical interactions for policy improvement is costly. Offline reinforcement learning provides a natural way to reuse collected experience, while flow-based policies offer an additional opportunity because fresh generation trajectories can be cheaply resampled from the current policy at replay observations. This motivates separating offline value learning from on-policy optimization of the generative process. We propose OVOGO, an offline reinforcement learning framework that realizes this separation for flow-based VLAs. A central challenge in this formulation is to learn a critic that remains grounded in logged robot experience while providing useful value s"

source = "arXiv"
summary = "用双模型分工和缓存感知压缩控制长程交互历史，降低14.5%至28.0%的端到端成本并提升任务表现。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30412"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：长程智能体反复输入持续增长的交互轨迹，导致输入量和调用成本不断上升；前缀缓存只能复用计算，无法抑制上下文增长或删除与当前决策无关的信息。\n- **旧法为何失效**：选择性压缩容易丢失全局信息，递归改写摘要会产生语义漂移，而反复压缩完整历史又会把不断增长的计算成本转移给压缩模型。\n- **方法**：DMC-5让通用压缩模型整合完整历史、专用智能体生成动作；以只追加的Canonical View保存原始轨迹并稳定复用历史前缀，以紧凑Decision View向智能体提供摘要和近期原始交互，并通过YAMATO保留工具操作所需的执行关键信息。\n- **实验**：在AppWorld、OfficeBench和8-objective QA上，智能体输入量减少38.5%至57.0%，端到端成本降低14.5%至28.0%，压缩模型缓存命中率达78.0%至85.0%；相较未压缩基线，任务表现提升1.0%至5.8%。"
purpose = "在长程工具调用或办公智能体系统中借鉴Canonical View与Decision View双视图机制，压缩历史输入并复用前缀缓存以降低运行成本。"
insight = "可靠的长程上下文压缩不应反复覆写唯一摘要，而应分离可缓存的完整记录与面向当前决策的紧凑视图，以兼顾信息完整性、语义稳定性和低成本。"
notes = "标题：DMC-5: Dual-Model Collaboration with Cache-aware Context Compression for Long-horizon Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30412\n摘要：Long-horizon agents repeatedly feed an ever-growing interaction trajectory to a specialized agent model. Prefix caching reuses computation over repeated history, but cannot prevent context growth or remove information irrelevant to the current decision. This is especially costly when the specialized agent lacks the cached-input discounts available to general-purpose models, making trajectory compression a natural alternative. However, existing compression methods on selective context risk losing global information, recursively rewriting summary leads to semantic drift, while repeatedly recompressing the full history shifts the growing cost to the compressor. Therefore, we introduce DMC-5 (Dual-Model Collaboration with CaChe-aware Context Compression), an asymmetric framework that assigns f"


[[items]]
id = "vopd"
title = "VOPD：验证器引导的稠密蒸馏"
subtopic = "在策略蒸馏"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "把稀疏的可验证奖励转化为覆盖全词表的稠密监督，提升数学推理模型的训练效率与效果。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30325"
code = "https://github.com/juntongshi48/VOPD"
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：RLVR只有轨迹级验证奖励，无法直接监督未采样的下一词动作；在策略蒸馏虽能提供全词表反馈，却不保证与任务结果一致。\n- **旧法为何失效**：简单联合优化RLVR与在策略蒸馏并未真正耦合两类信号，稀疏验证反馈仍无法影响教师提供的全分布监督。\n- **方法**：VOPD在隐式奖励空间中统一教师监督与验证器反馈，将验证器偏好投影到教师的隐式奖励方向，再通过Rao–Blackwell化扩展至完整词表；也可视为根据教师与验证器的一致程度，自适应外推蒸馏目标。\n- **实验**：在两种教师—学生配置和八个数学推理基准上，VOPD相较普通OPD及其他在策略蒸馏基线持续提升最终性能与学习效率。"
purpose = "在数学推理模型后训练中直接使用其代码，或借鉴验证器偏好投影机制，将可验证奖励接入在策略蒸馏并转化为稠密词元级信号。"
insight = "稀疏结果奖励与稠密教师反馈只有统一到同一奖励空间，才能让任务结果有效约束包括未采样动作在内的完整策略分布。"
notes = "标题：Verifiable On-Policy Distillation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30325\n摘要：Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a powerful approach for improving language-model reasoning, but its supervision is inherently sparse: a sequence-level verifier reward is propagated only through sampled trajectories and provides no direct signal for alternative actions. On-policy distillation (OPD) offers a complementary form of supervision by providing dense teacher feedback over the full next-token distribution, but this signal is not grounded in verified task outcomes. Simply co-optimizing the two objectives does not couple these signals: verifier feedback remains sparse and does not inform the dense teacher supervision. We introduce Verifiable On-Policy Distillation (VOPD), which transfers sparse verifier feedback into dense, verifier-grounded supervisio"

source = "arXiv"
summary = "用任务感知的多模态经验固化保留跨记录推理结果，避免智能体在相似问题上重复犯错。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30287"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：智能体长期积累对话、图像和文档后，需要跨记录关联证据回答问题，但已完成任务形成的理解通常不会被保留，导致相似错误反复出现。\n- **旧法为何失效**：现有多模态记忆主要存储和检索原始记录，缺少从已完成任务中提炼经验并按任务需求固化的机制。\n- **方法**：MMMem采用四类互补策略：用来源关联的多视图记忆组织文本、图像、渲染页面及融合线索的场景图；通过来源生成任务进行记忆预热和自训练；由同一视觉语言模型蒸馏任务上下文、结论与证据链接；在回答和验证时联合检索来源视图与相关经验，并以来源支持的复用促进长期保留。\n- **实验**：在LoCoMo、Mem-Gallery和LooGLE v2三个记忆基准、两种视觉语言读取器上超过八个基线；搭配Qwen3.5-9B时，相对最佳基线分别提升36.9%、17.6%和15.2%，消融实验表明训练与记忆预热均带来稳定增益。"
purpose = "在需要持续处理用户对话、图片和文档的长期智能体中，借鉴其任务经验蒸馏与来源关联检索机制构建可复用的多模态记忆。"
insight = "长期记忆的关键不只是保存原始记录，而是将任务结论连同证据来源固化为可验证经验，并同步对齐外部记忆与参数知识。"
notes = "标题：MMMem: Self-Improving Agents with Task-Aware Multimodal Experience Consolidation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30287\n摘要：AI agents accumulate conversations, images, and documents through long-term interactions with users. Answering later questions often requires connecting evidence across these records, yet the resulting understanding is rarely retained, allowing mistakes to recur on related questions. Existing multimodal memory methods largely focus on storing and retrieving records, with limited mechanisms for learning from completed tasks and consolidating task-specific experience. We therefore propose MMMem, a task-aware multimodal memory framework that extracts and consolidates experience from completed tasks through four complementary strategies. First, source-linked multi-view memory organizes original text, images, and rendered pages alongside generated scene images that combine clues across records "

summary = "用轨迹蒸馏提高独立记忆模块的兼容性，解决长期助手无需联合训练的协同难题，准确率达73.1%。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30230"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：长期运行的网页助手需要从历史交互中写入并找回信息，但在不同环境中独立训练的记忆与检索专家能否直接组合尚不明确。\n- **旧法为何失效**：现有强化学习专家虽能单独生效，但组合后兼容性不足，在基于 LongMemEval 的基准上准确率仅为 36.2%。\n- **方法**：从前沿模型的动作轨迹中分别蒸馏记忆写入策略和顺序检索策略，将其训练为 3B 模型，再与固定答案生成器组合，全程不做下游联合训练。\n- **实验**：蒸馏组合在 LongMemEval 基准上达到 73.1%，任务适配后经强化学习升至 75.2%；在 PersonaMem-v2 上也优于所评测的强化学习组合，经监督蒸馏适配后达到 48.9%。\n- **局限与后续**：消融显示单个策略的改进效果取决于组合对象，模块化系统仍需专门评估并提升不同专家间的兼容性。"
purpose = "在长期对话或网页助手中，可借鉴轨迹蒸馏机制，将独立训练的记忆写入器和顺序检索器模块化接入固定答案生成器。"
insight = "专用策略的单项能力并不保证组合收益，行为接口的兼容性才是模块化长期智能体有效迁移与协作的关键。"
notes = "标题：Composing Independently Distilled Memory and Retrieval Policies for Long-Term Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30230\n摘要：Long-lived Web assistants require effective memory and retrieval mechanisms to access information from past user interactions. While these capabilities can be learned through specialized policies, it remains unclear whether specialists trained independently in different environments can be effectively composed without joint optimization. This paper investigates compositional transfer through policy distillation. Memory-writing and sequential retrieval policies are independently distilled from frontier-model action trajectories into 3B models and combined with a fixed answer generator without downstream training. On a LongMemEval-based benchmark, the distilled composition achieves 73.1% accuracy, compared with 36.2% for a composition of existing RL-trained specialists, and also outperforms "

id = "roboada"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "保持冻结策略与计划不变，用运动反馈在线校正不可见物理变化造成的指令失准，成功率由27.8%升至62.9%。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30209"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：部署环境中的物体重量、关节性能或控制延迟可能发生不可见变化，使视觉策略仍能生成正确计划，却因指令与实际运动失配而执行失败。\n- **旧法为何失效**：预先训练难以覆盖未知物理变化；部署后修改策略可能破坏原计划，而基于梯度的机器人模型更新缺乏可靠性判断，甚至会在物理条件未变化时降低性能。\n- **方法**：RoboAda冻结原策略并保持计划不变，在每个动作块后用原机器人逆动力学模型将观测运动转换为“原系统所需指令”，形成免费在线标签；再按标签预测准确度加权多种机器人响应假设，包括“物理未变化”假设，据此校正下一动作块的指令，全程无需奖励、梯度更新或变化后机器人的预采集数据。\n- **实验**：在LIBERO上评测5个冻结策略，包括2个世界动作模型和3个视觉—语言—动作模型，覆盖11种物理偏移，平均成功率由27.8%升至62.9%；在Cosmos Policy上超过7种测试时适应方法中的最佳结果17.3个百分点，并基本保持物理条件不变时的性能。RoboTwin 2.0双臂关节空间实验中，关节目标偏移下成功率由24.9%升至78.8%。"
purpose = "在存在未知载荷、关节退化或控制延迟的机器人系统中，借鉴其逆动力学自标注与响应假设加权机制，为冻结策略增加无需重训的指令校正层。"
insight = "物理偏移下的高层计划仍可能正确，因此适应应优先放在计划与控制指令的接口层，以免为修正执行误差而破坏原有决策能力。"
notes = "标题：RoboAda: Adapting Frozen Robot Policies at Test Time to Physics They Cannot See\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30209\n摘要：Pretrained robot policies decide what to do from camera images, yet some of the physics that decides whether their plans succeed is not visible. An object may look light but be 100 times heavier, and a weakened joint or a lagging controller leaves no trace in the image. When the physics at deployment differs from training, the plan is often still right but is executed as if nothing had changed. Training on such variations in advance cannot handle unseen changes, while adapting once deployed either changes the plan, leaving the miscalibrated commands in place, or updates a robot model by gradient steps that do not know how reliable they are and can make the robot worse even when nothing has changed. We propose RoboAda, which keeps the policy and its plan unchanged and corrects the commands "

summary = "从专业公开资料自动合成带逐项评分契约的可验证任务，降低法律、金融等领域的专家出题成本。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30202"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：法律、金融等专业分析任务答案开放，难以自动验证并转化为可规模扩展的训练与评测数据，高质量任务仍主要依赖专家编写。\n- **旧法为何失效**：软件工程任务可借助代码与可执行测试验证，但专业分析缺少类似判定机制，人工出题成本高、扩展慢。\n- **方法**：VADE利用“求解难、证据固定后验证易”的不对称性，从专业公开来源及其证据构建参考分析，再让看不到参考答案的独立智能体依据相同证据复现结论；验证通过后，将分析编译为带逐项评分契约的开放式任务。\n- **实验**：VADE覆盖四个专业领域；对186个金融任务的独立专家审查显示，93.5%同时满足来源忠实、答案正确和评估公平的高标准。用验证后的求解轨迹训练可提升留出任务表现，并迁移至Finance Agent Benchmark v2。"
purpose = "在法律、金融等专业智能体的训练与评测中，可借鉴证据固定、独立复现验证和逐项评分契约机制批量合成任务。"
insight = "开放式专业分析并非天然不可验证；固定来源证据后，求解与验证的难度差异可将复杂结论转化为可扩展的可靠监督。"
notes = "标题：VADE: A Verifiable Agent Data Engine from Professional Sources\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30202\n摘要：Language agents have shown their practical value, particularly in software engineering, where abundant public code and executable tests support training and evaluation at scale. Extending this success to other professional domains, such as law and finance, is not straightforward: many agent tasks involve open-ended analysis, making outcomes hard to verify and, in turn, hard to use as scalable supervision. As a result, high-quality tasks in these domains are still largely expert-authored, making them expensive to scale and limiting their use for training. To address this bottleneck, we introduce VADE, a data engine that automatically constructs verifiable analysis tasks from public sources used in professional practice. VADE exploits a simple asymmetry: professional analyses are difficult t"

source = "arXiv"
summary = "用可核查证据与成对标注直接估计网页智能体差距，避免评判规则制造或掩盖性能优势。\n\n[合并自 2026-11-28 same-executions-different-gaps]\n用证据约束判定与成对标签回归，解决网页智能体性能差距易被评判规则扭曲的问题。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30190"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：网页智能体通常按成功率排名，但排名无法说明性能优势有多可信；同一批执行轨迹可能因成功定义、证据要求和差距估计方式不同而产生截然不同的领先幅度。\n- **旧法为何失效**：仅预测任务是否可能完成会把缺少记录支持的结果计为成功；分别提高两个智能体的得分估计精度也可能因破坏原有误差抵消，使二者差距反而估得更不准。\n- **方法**：将“有证据支持的完成”“未确定完成”和“有证据支持的失败”分开，并结合评判器预测与抽样任务对的参考标签，通过成对回归直接估计目标性能差距。\n- **实验**：在四个已发布网页智能体执行系统上，将评判规则从预测可能完成改为要求记录证据后，大部分已报告领先幅度消失但排名不变；使用相同的50个已标注任务对拟合回归，比直接成对抽样的估计误差更低。\n\n[合并自 2026-11-28 same-executions-different-gaps]\n- **问题**：网页智能体通常按成功率排名，但排名无法说明性能差距是否可信；成功定义、证据要求和差距估计方式都会影响结论。\n- **旧法为何失效**：仅预测任务“可能完成”的评判器会把缺乏记录支持的执行判为成功；分别提高两个智能体的分数估计精度，也可能因破坏原有误差抵消而降低差值精度。\n- **方法**：要求成功判断有执行记录支持，并区分“无法确认完成”与“有证据支持的失败”；再结合抽样任务对的参考标签，直接估计选定结果指标上的智能体差距。\n- **实验**：在四个已发布的网页智能体执行系统上，切换为证据约束评判可消除大部分已报告领先幅度而不改变排名；使用相同50对标注样本拟合回归，比直接成对抽样的差距估计误差更低。"
purpose = "在网页智能体基准评测或版本对比中，借鉴其证据约束判定和成对标签回归机制，审计成功率提升是否真实可靠。\n\n[合并自 2026-11-28 same-executions-different-gaps]\n在网页智能体基准评测中保留可审计执行证据，并用少量成对标注直接估计系统差距，以复核性能提升声明。"
insight = "智能体间的相对差距不是两个绝对分数精度的自然结果，只有明确比较目标并直接校准差值，才能避免误差抵消造成的虚假优势。\n\n[合并自 2026-11-28 same-executions-different-gaps]\n智能体之间的性能差距应被视为独立估计目标；单体分数更准确并不保证差值更准确，可信比较必须直接围绕成对证据建模。"
notes = "标题：Same Executions, Different Gaps: Judgment Rules in Web-Agent Evaluation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30190\n摘要：Success-rate gains guide the development of web agents. Yet knowing which agent ranks first does not tell us how much confidence to place in its reported advantage. We study the comparison itself: how success is defined, what evidence supports the judgments, and how the difference between agents is estimated. On fixed web-agent executions, changing a judge from predicting likely completion to requiring recorded support can remove most of a reported lead without changing the ranking. This effect persists when unresolved completion is distinguished from supported failure. Controlled source additions explain how missing relations between task requirements, objects and observations can conceal a difference or create an apparent advantage. We also find that making both agents' score estimates m"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "用三种评估接口拆分语言模型遗忘来源，发现许多性能下降源于输出头与骨干错配，而非知识彻底丢失。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30151"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：持续学习通常只通过最终分类头或语言模型头衡量遗忘，无法判断性能下降来自骨干表征退化、输出头错配，还是两者共同作用。\n- **旧法为何失效**：把后向迁移与前向迁移视为模型级单一属性，会混淆部署性能、表征可恢复性和类别可分性，导致对灾难性遗忘程度的误判。\n- **方法**：建立三接口诊断：顺序更新的已训练输出头衡量部署性能，冻结骨干上新训练的探针衡量新读出下的可恢复性，无分类器骨干诊断衡量表征可分性；另提出仅微调语言模型头的 JLT 干预，以验证头—骨干错配。\n- **实验**：在编码器与解码器语言骨干的类别增量实验及任务增量检查中，已训练头显示严重遗忘，新探针显示接近完全恢复，骨干诊断显示中等表征漂移及稳定性与可塑性的持续负相关；结论覆盖最近类别均值诊断、5 种聚类算法和 3 种指标。JLT 为回放及蒸馏方法恢复了相对联合训练差距的 96%–99%，对普通顺序微调约恢复四分之三。\n- **局限与后续**：后续持续学习评测需明确遗忘所对应的评估接口，而不能只报告单一遗忘数值。"
purpose = "在语言模型持续学习系统中同时部署已训练头、新探针和无分类器骨干诊断，并用 JLT 判断性能下降能否通过重新校准输出头修复。"
insight = "遗忘与迁移并非脱离评估接口的模型固有属性，许多所谓灾难性遗忘其实是可恢复表征上的读出错配，仅有部分源于真实表征漂移。"
notes = "标题：Revisiting Continual Learning in Language Models: A Three-Interface Diagnosis of Stability and Plasticity\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30151\n摘要：Continual learning in language models is commonly studied in the incremental learning (IL) setting, where a model learns a sequence of tasks and is evaluated through its final classifier or language-model (LM) head. This interface, however, does not indicate where forgetting occurs: a drop in accuracy may reflect representation degradation in the backbone, readout mismatch in the head, or both. We therefore argue that backward transfer (BWT) and forward transfer (FWT) should be interpreted as interface-dependent quantities rather than as single modellevel properties. We study three evaluation interfaces: the trained head, which is updated sequentially with the backbone; a probe newly trained on frozen backbone features; and a classifier-free backbone diagnostic. They measure deployed perfo"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "用配置、代码和前20%训练曲线预测后续走势，帮助科研智能体提前筛除不值得继续计算的训练任务。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30141"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：科研智能体通常将每次训练完整运行，缺乏研究者根据配置和早期曲线判断后续走势、及时停止无望实验的能力。\n- **旧法为何失效**：传统曲线拟合主要依赖数值趋势，难以理解代码改动和设计选择对训练动态的影响；日志稀疏时，各类预测器的能力都会显著下降。\n- **方法**：构建 Tea Leaves 基准，汇集预训练、微调和强化学习中的682次真实训练；预测器读取配置、描述、代码及前20%曲线，预测剩余训练走势，并可参考相关历史运行。\n- **实验**：评测11个LLM及多种预测基线；最强LLM在平滑损失曲线上的技能分为0.66，高于最佳曲线拟合器的0.62，在噪声较大的强化学习奖励曲线上为0.43，对比基线0.13。提示中加入相关运行可将损失曲线预测误差降低39%，最强LLM还能正确排序全部36对仅有代码改动差异的运行。\n- **局限与后续**：在日志稀疏的社区训练记录上，所有方法均损失大部分预测能力，且尚无方法能可靠提前识别失败运行；单次科研智能体运行的预测也未优于直接采用最新观测值。"
purpose = "在自动化训练或科研智能体系统中，将 Tea Leaves 作为训练走势预测基准，并借鉴其早期曲线读取机制调度算力和提前终止低潜力运行。"
insight = "训练走势预测的关键不只是拟合曲线形状，而是结合代码变更与历史运行理解设计选择的因果影响；高质量日志比增加工具或推理投入更重要。"
notes = "标题：Reading the Tea Leaves, Again: Can Large Language Models Predict Language Model Training Runs?\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30141\n摘要：LLM agents now propose research ideas and run the experiments that test them, but they train every run to its end, whereas a researcher reads a run's configuration and the beginning of its curve and stops the runs that show no promise. Reading a run this way takes more than describing or implementing a training method: it takes knowing what a design choice does to a run. We build Tea Leaves to measure this knowledge on 682 real training runs across pretraining, fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning (RL): a forecaster sees the first 20% of a run with its configuration, description, and code, and predicts the rest. Across 11 LLMs and a range of forecasting baselines, the strongest LLMs match or exceed the best curve fitter on smooth loss curves (skill 0.66 against 0.62) and lead on noisy R"

summary = "将求解器性能缺口追溯到开发决策并演化通用指导，让组合优化经验跨问题复用，TSP 差距降至 0.52%。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30139"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：通用 LLM 智能体虽能开发完整的组合优化求解器，但开发流程通常针对单一问题定制，经验难以迁移到新问题。\n- **旧法为何失效**：外部评估只衡量最终求解器，而技能指导作用于中间开发决策，性能反馈难以准确归因到指导中的具体缺陷。\n- **方法**：AutoSECO 沿求解器代码和已记录的开发决策回溯性能缺口，将缺陷修订为通用指令；仅当新智能体依据修订技能重新开发的求解器降低平均评估差距时，才保留修订。\n- **实验**：技能仅在旅行商问题上演化，随后冻结并迁移至容量车辆路径、作业车间调度和三维装箱问题；其 TSP 平均基准差距为 0.52%，优于 SkillOpt 的 0.98%，四类问题的汇总表现也优于无技能、初始技能和 SkillOpt，并能适配不同模型与智能体脚手架组合。"
purpose = "在组合优化求解器开发智能体中借鉴其性能归因与修订验收机制，自动沉淀可迁移到新问题的开发指导。"
insight = "跨任务迁移的关键资产不是具体求解器，而是从性能缺口中归因、并经重新开发验证的通用决策原则。"
notes = "标题：AutoSECO: Automated Skill Evolution for Cross-Problem Combinatorial Optimization with General-Purpose LLM Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30139\n摘要：General-purpose large language model (LLM) agents can develop complete solvers for combinatorial optimization problems. Their specialization, however, often relies on workflows engineered for individual problems, limiting the reuse of development expertise. We propose AutoSECO, an automated skill evolution method that learns reusable solver-development guidance from experience. The challenge is that external evaluation measures the completed solver, while the skill guides decisions during development. AutoSECO connects this feedback to guidance deficiencies by tracing performance shortfalls through solver code and recorded development decisions. Revisions are expressed as general instructions and retained only if a fresh solver developed under the revised skill achieves a lower mean evalua"

insight = "把不断变化的用户偏好压缩为可递归更新的紧凑状态，可以在不保存或重放完整历史的情况下实现恒定成本的持续个性化。"
notes = "标题：WASP: Continual Weight Adaptation for Streaming LLM Personalization\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30134\n摘要：Personalized large language models (LLMs) should continually adapt to users' evolving preferences. We introduce WASP (Weight Adaptation for Streaming Personalization), a recurrent framework that captures fine-grained preferences directly from raw user interactions and generates personalized LoRA adapters without expensive per-user optimization. Specifically, WASP achieves this by training a shared hypernetwork that maintains a compact user representation and outputs adapter weights in a single forward pass. As new interactions arrive, WASP updates this representation without reprocessing full interaction history, enabling continual adaptation with per-update costs in"

subtopic = "机制发现"

summary = "从任务结构预先算出网络实现泛化所需的低维子空间，揭示延迟泛化源于表征搜索而非优化噪声。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30116"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：神经网络记住训练数据后为何要等待很久才突然泛化，且能否在训练前预测其最终表征。\n- **旧法为何失效**：既有解释无法提前指出网络将收敛到何种表征，因此不同假说难以相互检验或证伪。\n- **方法**：从群任务本身计算一个本质上唯一的低维嵌入子空间，即“正确子空间”，并将 Grokking 的延迟解释为网络搜索该子空间所需的时间。\n- **实验**：预先提供正确子空间会消除延迟；移除它后准确率止于训练前即可计算的上限，乘法任务完全无法泛化。常规训练中，嵌入会在测试准确率上升前主动靠近该子空间；干预实验表明搜索过程而非优化噪声决定延迟长度。"
purpose = "在群运算学习或 Grokking 实验中，可借鉴正确子空间的预计算与干预机制，用于初始化表征、预测性能上限或诊断延迟泛化。"
insight = "延迟泛化的关键不是网络何时摆脱记忆或优化噪声，而是其表征何时找到由任务代数结构预先决定的几何位置。"
notes = "标题：Grokking as a Subspace Search: Computing the Destination Before Training\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30116\n摘要：Grokking is the phenomenon in which a neural network generalizes long after it has memorized its training data. Many explanations have been proposed for the delay between memorization and generalization. However, none of them can say before training where the network is heading, so they cannot be tested against one another. We show that on group tasks this destination can be computed from the task itself before training begins. It is a low-dimensional subspace of the network's embeddings, which we call the right subspace, and we prove that it is essentially unique. Knowing the destination makes our hypothesis falsifiable: the delay is a search for the right subspace, and generalization begins when the network finds it. When we give the network the right subspace from the start, the delay d"

content = "- **问题**：智能体从显式技能说明中学习后，推理时即使不再提供技能，仍可能引用已移除内容，形成上下文错位（CoMi）。\n- **旧法为何失效**：常规流程用带技能上下文生成示范轨迹，再移除技能微调同一模型，却未清除轨迹中依赖原技能的表述，导致模型依赖部署时不可见的信息。\n- **方法**：CGSI 使用句子检测器定位缺乏当前上下文支撑的引用，并在微调前掩码或改写相关句子，使训练轨迹与推理时可见的上下文一致。\n- **实验**：在 ALFWorld、WebShop 和 ARC-AGI-1 上，直接移除技能微调仍有 12.2%–46.0% 的测试轨迹引用已删除技能；CGSI 在保持任务成功率的同时将 CoMi 降低 72.5%–94.6%，以更短训练时间达到与强化学习式技能内化基线相当的表现，处理成本也低于大规模轨迹审计与纠正。"
purpose = "在智能体的技能蒸馏或轨迹微调流程中，用 CGSI 清理自生成轨迹中的失据引用，再将外部技能内化到模型参数中。"
insight = "技能是否真正内化不仅取决于任务成功率，更取决于训练轨迹的推理依据能否被部署时实际可见的上下文支持。"
notes = "标题：Context-Grounded Skill Internalization for Vision-Language Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30075\n摘要：Skill internalization offers a way for agents to learn useful behaviors from skills and use them without explicit guidance in context at inference time. A common approach is to generate demonstration trajectories with the skill in context and then fine-tune the same model on its own trajectories with the skill removed. However, this leads to the problem that the model may learn to reference the removed skill, which we named as context misalignment (CoMi). In this work, we first analyze agent behaviors related to CoMi under this common pipeline. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and ARC-AGI-1, skill-removed fine-tuning consistently results in CoMi: 12.2-46.0% of test trajectories reference the removed skill. To address this problem, we develop Context-Grounded Skill Internalization (CGSI). CGSI use"

summary = "用暂停与持续运行的配对任务评测动态环境记忆，机器人成功率从97%降至50%。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30047"

content = "- **问题**：真实环境不会在机器人思考时暂停，先前观察可能在行动前失效，但现有基准难以检验智能体能否依据最新状态行动。\n- **旧法为何失效**：传统基准在决策期间暂停模拟器，使记忆内容不会过期，掩盖了模型看见变化却仍依据旧画面行动的问题。\n- **方法**：RoboLiveMem让同一记忆任务分别在世界暂停和持续运行时执行，涵盖三档29类任务、16个BEHAVIOR-1K真实场景及ManiSkill受控任务，并混合真实变化、无害相似变化和无变化样本；可见变化的孪生任务用于区分遗忘与行动过迟。\n- **实验**：在16个模型、34,176个回合上，8个Claude和GPT模型在真实场景对象变化任务中的平均成功率由97%降至50%，Claude Opus 5.5由100%降至44%；即使每次决策限时2秒，运行设置仍至少落后36个百分点。\n- **实验**：行动前重新观察可弥合大部分差距，但在相同物体多次交换时，测试的9个模型仍出现明显的记忆失分。"
purpose = "在具身智能体评测中直接使用RoboLiveMem的暂停/运行配对任务，并借鉴行动前重新观察机制识别和减少状态过期导致的失败。"
insight = "可靠的机器人记忆不仅取决于能否保存信息，更取决于行动时所依据的信息是否仍然有效。"
notes = "标题：RoboLiveMem: Is Robot Memory Solved When the World Does Not Wait?\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30047\n摘要：Robot agents built on frontier vision-language models now solve most robot memory benchmarks. Does this mean they can remember what they need in a home, where people and machines keep moving things? Existing memory benchmarks cannot answer this question, because they pause the simulator while the model thinks: nothing can change during a decision, so whatever the robot remembers is still true when it acts. We introduce RoboLiveMem, a benchmark that runs the same memory tasks twice, once with the world paused while the model thinks and once with the world running. It contains 29 task families in three tiers, from 16 realistic BEHAVIOR-1K scenes of homes, workshops and a restaurant to controlled ManiSkill tasks. Every task mixes real changes with harmless look-alikes and runs without any cha"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "用严格隔离172个工具经验与训练数据的评测，揭示共享经验会挤出未见工具候选，使路由准确率从0.534降至0.278。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30034"
code = "https://github.com/<org>/STUN-Bench"
dataset = "https://github.com/<org>/STUN-Bench"
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：评估跨组织共享历史请求与工具使用经验能否帮助路由器识别真正未见的工具，避免测试集泄漏掩盖泛化缺陷。\n- **旧法为何失效**：StableToolBench所谓“未见工具”的测试查询及正确工具标签已存在于ToolBench指令池中，使检索器和经验共享方法可间接接触答案，甚至反转方法优劣结论。\n- **方法**：构建STUN-Bench，从共享经验池和检索器训练数据中彻底移除所有命名或标注172个留出工具的样本，并提供泄漏过滤器与评测框架。\n- **实验**：五个模拟客户端的共享经验会产生检索“拥挤效应”，使未被使用的工具描述跌出候选列表；使用Jina-v2时，留出工具路由准确率从0.534降至0.278。工具检索专用检索器降幅较小，将经验与工具描述分库存储可恢复大部分损失；在无泄漏划分上，共享经验的效果由优于纯工具描述反转为更差。\n- **局限与后续**：这里的“未见”仅指工具未出现在共享经验和检索器训练数据中，不代表其未进入语言模型预训练语料。"
purpose = "在工具路由或多组织经验共享系统中，直接使用STUN-Bench及其泄漏过滤器审计真实泛化能力，并将经验与工具描述分库存储作为抗候选挤占基线。"
insight = "当经验与工具描述竞争同一检索排名时，热门工具经验会系统性排挤未见工具，因此共享经验的价值必须在严格排除泄漏与检索拥挤后判断。"
notes = "标题：STUN-Bench: Truly Unseen Tools for Evaluating Shared Tool Experience\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30034\n摘要：A tool router retrieves a few candidate tools per request and lets a language model choose. Methods that share experience across organizations add past requests about each tool to what the router retrieves. StableToolBench calls some of its test groups tests on \"unseen\" tools, yet the ToolBench instruction pool, which retrievers and experience-sharing methods learn from, contains every test query and labels every gold tool of the two single-tool groups among them. We release STUN-Bench (StableToolBench, Truly UNseen), a split whose experience pool has no item that labels or names 172 held-out tools. Unseen here means absent from shared experience and retriever training data, not from pretraining. On the split we find crowding: experience pooled from five simulated clients, added to an inde"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "保留物品的原始观测历史，让家庭机器人适应会消失后重现的生活惯例，优于仅维护文字摘要。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29818"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：家庭中的物品位置会随住户生病、康复等生活惯例反复改变，机器人既要及时适应新规律，也不能遗忘未来可能再次有用的旧经验。\n- **旧法为何失效**：现有记忆评测多采用静态环境；仅保存语言模型生成的文字摘要，虽能记录物品去向，却很少区分病中模式与日常惯例，容易丢失可复用信息。\n- **方法**：在模拟家庭的生病、康复、再次生病过程中，对比保留物品全部出现房间的规则、仅存文字摘要的语言模型记忆，以及可读取原始观测历史的语言模型记忆。\n- **实验**：简单的历史保留规则优于所有仅摘要方案；向语言模型开放原始历史后差距消失，而在历史之上额外生成摘要没有可测收益。病情再次出现时，保留历史的方法搜索表现优于首次；删除首次生病记录会消除规则方法的全部增益，并削弱其他历史方法。\n- **局限与后续**：结果来自模拟家庭，仍需长期机器人部署基准，专门检验系统能否保留并复用可能再次出现的生活惯例。"
purpose = "在长期运行的家庭机器人中保留并检索原始物品观测历史，并将动态惯例设置作为记忆更新与持续学习方案的评测基线。"
insight = "长期记忆的关键不是只压缩当前事实，而是保存带情境的历史证据，使暂时消失的旧惯例重现时仍可复用。"
notes = "标题：How Should a Home Robot Update Its Memories When Human Routines Change?\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29818\n摘要：A home robot that helps with chores needs to know where objects are, but people move objects according to routines that change and later change back. The robot's memory must adapt to the new routine without erasing experience that may become useful again. One approach is to have a language model maintain written summaries of the home from the robot's own observations, but existing evaluations of such memories use static worlds. We explore simulated households where a resident gets sick, recovers, and later gets sick again. We compare a rule that keeps every room an object has been seen in, language-model memories that store only written summaries, and language-model memories that can also read the raw history of sightings. The rule outperforms every summary-only memory, and giving language"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "用正常运行轨迹构建独立监控器，解决自改进智能体难以察觉环境变化故障的问题，在3个领域捕获多数故障。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29807"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：智能体可通过分析失败并修改程序实现递归自我改进，但长期自主运行时必须先可靠发现环境变化引发的故障，才能触发修复。\n- **旧法为何失效**：仅看任务得分会掩盖故障发现能力；智能体自行编写的运行时检查未审查真正暴露异常的记录关系，即使程序得分很高，也可能不报告实际失败。\n- **方法**：利用正常运行记录构建独立监控器，报警后再次调用智能体修复；同时测试明确告知智能体“检查器是唯一回调机制”能否改善检查质量。\n- **实验**：在5个任务领域、最多7种代码编写模型上，原有检查在4个领域漏掉至少一半故障；正常记录监控器在3个领域捕获多数故障，智能体被唤回后通常能完成修复，最强智能体在获知唯一回调约束后也能写出捕获多数故障的检查器。\n- **局限与后续**：后续自我改进评测不应只衡量最终得分，还应测量系统发现并报告自身故障的能力。"
purpose = "在长期自主智能体系统中，将基于正常轨迹的独立监控器接入回调链路，用于发现环境变化故障、触发代码修复并评测故障发现率。"
insight = "递归自我改进能否形成闭环，关键瓶颈往往不是修复能力，而是系统能否可靠判断何时需要启动修复。"
notes = "标题：Blind to What They Can Fix: A Detection Gap in Self-Improving Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29807\n摘要：AI agents can write programs that carry out a task without calling a model at every step, so the task runs fast and its logic stays open to inspection. Agents improve these programs by running them, studying their failures, and revising the code, a form of recursive self-improvement (RSI). We ask whether this process also yields runtime checks that can be trusted. In five task domains, with up to seven author models, agents bring their programs to high scores. Yet when the environment changes, the checks they were told to write stay silent on half or more of the resulting failures in four domains. These failures can be detected and repaired: a monitor built from records of normal runs catches most of them in three domains, and the same agents usually repair them once called. The checks do "

score = 3

summary = "用统一概率评分和闭环游戏比较快反模型与生成式模型，发现单次前向通常更准、校准更好且延迟更低。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29773"
code = "https://github.com/zhaoyang02/S1Arena-MM"
dataset = "https://github.com/zhaoyang02/S1Arena-MM"
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：判断哪些图像、视频和音频离散决策可交给单次前向的 System-1 模型，哪些仍需生成文本推理的多模态语言模型。\n- **旧法为何失效**：两类模型分别输出选项概率和文本，既有评测难以在统一请求与概率评分下公平比较，也较少考察实时闭环决策。\n- **方法**：S1Arena-MM 以相同决策请求和概率评分评测所有模型，覆盖来自公开及3个程序生成数据集的94项任务、51,060个问题，并加入102个基于截图交互的闭环游戏环境。\n- **实验**：评测12个 System-1 模型和21个开源 MLLM；最佳 System-1 在图像任务上领先最佳 MLLM 4.9个百分点，视频和音频差距不足1个百分点，视频任务12组配对中决策后训练模型有10组取得更低的 Brier 分数。允许最多2,048个思考词元后，最强 MLLM 仅小幅反超且多数模型校准变差；BALROG 中3个基于27B MLLM的 System-1 模型取得22.0%至33.3%进度，其他关闭思考的模型均不超过9.7%，GameWorld 中每步至多1.1秒延迟未造成可测进度损失。"
purpose = "可在多模态分类、风险识别或实时游戏智能体中直接使用其代码和数据，对比单次前向与生成式模型的准确率、概率校准、闭环执行能力和实时延迟。"
insight = "多模态离散决策的关键不是生成更长的文字推理，而是直接学得校准良好的选项概率，从而兼顾闭环交互中的速度与决策质量。"
notes = "标题：S1Arena-MM: Which Multimodal Decisions Can Be Left to System 1?\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29773\n摘要：Perception systems often make discrete decisions: flag a hateful meme, predict whether a pedestrian will cross, detect a spoofed voice, or select the next move in a game. System-1 models, such as OneJev and Jev-Omni, read the media and return probabilities over the options in a single forward pass, while multimodal large language models (MLLMs) generate text. Which multimodal decisions can System-1 models handle, and where do MLLMs perform better? We introduce S1Arena-MM, a benchmark that gives every model the same decision requests and scores the probabilities it returns. It contains 51,060 questions across 94 tasks with image, video, and audio input, drawn from 94 public datasets and three program-generated ones. It also includes 102 closed-loop game environments that a model plays from "

content = "- **问题**：固定提示的软件开发智能体会在不同PR中重复犯错，但持续向指令文件追加经验规则会造成规则膨胀，且未必提高任务成功率。\n- **旧法为何失效**：冻结提示无法吸收历史教训，无治理的规则追加则缺少证据审核、效果验证与淘汰机制；规则有证据支撑也不代表能泛化。\n- **方法**：在四阶段LLM流水线中，每处理10个PR由Retrospective智能体提出规则修改，再由Notary依据证据批准、合并或拒绝，并将决策写入可溯源账本。\n- **实验**：预注册实验覆盖两个Python代码仓库的880个PR；Notary批准89项提案中的55项，并拒绝8项引用不存在证据的提案，但经过10轮回顾后，留出审查意见与隐藏测试修复率均未提升。\n- **局限与后续**：审查信号仅为每个PR平均0.45条评论，可学习信息过于稀疏；后续应将每条规则视为可检验假设，依据留出效果决定准入与淘汰。"
purpose = "在代码开发智能体或PR处理流水线中借鉴Notary门控与溯源账本，将其作为经验规则准入、验证和淘汰的基线。"
insight = "经验规则的证据可信度与泛化效用是两个独立问题，规则治理必须将来源审核和留出效果验证分开。"
notes = "标题：Admitted Is Not Effective: Lessons from a Notary-Gated Retrospective Loop in an Agentic Pipeline\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29727\n摘要：Agentic software development pipelines with frozen prompts can repeat the same mistakes across pull requests (PRs), yet ungoverned additions to instruction files grow without bound and do not generally improve task success. We present a retrospective loop for a four-stage large language model (LLM) pipeline in which a Retrospective agent proposes edits to learned rules every ten PRs and a Notary admits, merges, or rejects them while recording each decision in a provenance ledger. In a pre-registered study of 880 PRs on two Python repositories, the loop ran end to end and the Notary admitted 55 of 89 proposals, rejecting 8 that cited nonexistent evidence. However, held-out review comments and hidden-test resolution did not improve after ten retrospectives. Tracing each step of the loop, we "

content = "- **问题**：金融软件分散在接口和规范各异的库中，智能体难以发现、组合并可靠使用市场分析、预测、投资组合构建和交易研究工具。\n- **旧法为何失效**：不同模型和智能体框架需要重复构建工具集成，生成的金融程序也缺少统一检查与反馈机制，导致有效程序比例偏低。\n- **方法**：Fin-RSI 以共享接口统一金融技能与检查工具，支持智能体根据自然语言请求发现工具、复用经过测试的实现，并通过检查反馈持续改进生成程序；开发者也可直接扩展新工具。\n- **实验**：在20个开放权重模型和6项交易头寸任务上，工具发现将产出有效程序的回合占比从9.7%提高到29.2%；对参数量至少140亿的模型，该指标从23.6%升至64.6%。\n- **局限与后续**：当前工具和任务覆盖仍有限，作者计划随金融智能体发展继续扩充技能库与基准。"

score = 3

summary = "用覆盖繁殖、遗传与选择的种群级安全证书，解决个体均合规但群体演化后仍可能失控的问题。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31312"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：开放多智能体系统中的智能体会复制自身、修改后代并动态加入或退出；即使每个个体都通过安全检查，种群仍可能突破整体安全限制。\n- **旧法为何失效**：现有验证通常假设成员固定或已注册，无法覆盖持续产生后代、遗传修改、规则切换及谱系交互和选择所引发的群体风险。\n- **方法**：建立覆盖繁殖、遗传与选择的种群级形式化框架，给出安全保证持续成立的条件；证书可约束期望累计危害和所有可达种群状态，确保后代继承繁殖限制，并适用于根据历史自适应调整规则的调度器。\n- **实验**：四类反例表明，单个未授权副本平均产生少于一个后代时总量仍可无限增长；两个独立衰退的谱系交互后可指数增长，交替采用各自安全的抑制性规则也可能导致增长，选择还会使种群平均违规繁殖率超过认证上限。"
purpose = "在允许智能体自复制和修改后代的开放多智能体系统中，部署前借鉴该形式化证书验证繁殖控制、跨代继承约束、动态调度策略与累计危害上界。"
insight = "个体安全约束在成员构成内生变化时不具备组合闭包性，安全保证必须直接约束种群动力学及规则的跨代继承。"
notes = "标题：Evolutionary AI Control: Population-Level Safety Under Reproduction, Heredity, and Selection\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31312\n摘要：An AI agent can satisfy every individual safety check and still give rise to a population that violates a collective safety limit. This risk arises in open multi-agent systems, where agents copy themselves, modify their successors, and change system membership during a run. Selection then shifts the population toward traits outside the developers' optimization targets. We prove conditions under which safety guarantees persist through reproduction, inheritance, and selection, and we characterize how they fail. Four counterexamples illustrate these failures. Unauthorized copies, which violate a specified reproductive restriction, can each leave fewer than one successor on average while their expected number grows without bound under continued production from an expanding authorized populatio"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "用策略生成倾向与裁判误判率的乘积，在强化学习前预测最先被利用的奖励漏洞，12次利用均命中。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29651"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- **问题**：使用大语言模型裁判训练策略时，策略可能利用裁判漏洞获得高奖励，需要在强化学习开始前预测最可能先被利用的漏洞。\n- **旧法为何失效**：静态探测只能发现裁判会误接收单个“{”或“Thought process:”等退化答案，无法判断策略实际会先发现哪个缺陷；仅看裁判误报率也不充分。\n- **方法**：定义“暴露度”为初始策略生成某种漏洞模式的倾向与裁判误报率（FPR）的乘积，并通过拉丁方设计以三种剂量将漏洞模式植入微调数据，因果检验其预测能力。\n- **实验**：在7个裁判、36次GRPO运行中，发生漏洞利用的12次运行里，首个被利用模式均具有最高暴露度，而仅6次具有最高FPR；规则屏蔽一个模式后，训练前预测在4次中命中3次。仅植入两个模式且占微调数据3%至3.8%时，暴露度在6次运行中全部预测对首个漏洞及其出现窗口。\n- **局限与后续**：漏洞利用只在困难提示上出现，因为被裁判接受的漏洞答案会获得较大的组归一化优势。"
purpose = "在采用LLM裁判和GRPO训练策略的系统中，可在训练前计算候选漏洞的暴露度，优先审计、屏蔽高风险模式或修复裁判。"
insight = "奖励漏洞是否会被利用并非只由裁判缺陷决定，而取决于该缺陷与初始策略行为分布相遇的机会。"
notes = "标题：Which Loopholes Get Found? Predicting Reward Hacking of LLM Judges Before RL Training\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29651\n摘要：A policy trained against an LLM judge can learn to exploit its loopholes. Static probes show that such judges accept degenerate answers like a lone \"{\" or \"Thought process:\", but not which flaw a policy will find. In GRPO the first loophole to be exploited can be predicted before training from each pattern's exposure, the product of the starting policy's propensity to produce it and the judge's false-positive rate (FPR). To test exposure causally, we seed the patterns into fine-tuning at three doses in a Latin square. Across 36 GRPO runs with seven judges, the first exploited pattern had the highest exposure in all 12 runs with exploitation and the highest FPR in only 6. Predictions recorded before training held in three of four runs with one pattern masked by rule. When only two patterns "

id = "jko-grpo-wasserstein"
title = "JKO-GRPO：Wasserstein近端更新"
